# Agents · W · part 3

937 entries, web-researcher to websocket-mic-resync-fix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **web-researcher**: Web researcher with KB + Context7 pre-checks (covered topics skip web) and drift-guard question #5 on findings. Use when information is beyond training data — KB and Context7 run first to minimize unnecessary web calls. ([arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/codebase-intelligence/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Web research specialist. Fetches public documentation and pages, checks facts, and writes a sourced brief. Use for external library, tool, and practice research. ([asarchami/dotfiles](https://github.com/asarchami/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_pi/agent/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Searches the web and fetches URLs relevant to the LA wildfire vulnerability project. Use when looking for data sources, agency download pages, methodology references, or library documentation for geopandas / rasterio / osmnx / h3. Returns structured summaries, never raw page dumps. ([b0glarka/la-wildfire-vulnerability-index](https://github.com/b0glarka/la-wildfire-vulnerability-index/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Internet research for debugging, finding solutions, and gathering technical information. Searches GitHub issues, Stack Overflow, Reddit, forums, and documentation. ([broneq/bdk](https://github.com/broneq/bdk/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Gathers facts with sources for a reel - official figures, dates, names, quotes - from the web (official sites, news agencies, government pages first), and writes them as facts.md lines with a link per fact. Also finds free licensed media (Openverse, Pexels, archive.org) when asked. Several can run at once on different sub-questions. ([bunoor7/bunasser77](https://github.com/bunoor7/bunasser77/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Use for current external web research only when the graph also grants parent-active exa_search and exa_fetch extensionTools; catalog defaultTools are local-artifact reads only, not web access. ([ConditionedHair/pi](https://github.com/ConditionedHair/pi/blob/HEAD/vendor/packages/pi-multiagent/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Use this agent when you need to research general information, find articles, search for solutions, company research, or gather information from the web. Uses Exa MCP for intelligent web search. ([DobroslavRadosavljevic/ogie](https://github.com/DobroslavRadosavljevic/ogie/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Web Researcher on a CrelioBot team — finds what the KB and the model don't know (recent, specific, niche, prices, laws, tools) with the websearch skill at the right depth (fast, medium or deep), and reports with sources. ([IthonyGames/CrelioBot](https://github.com/IthonyGames/CrelioBot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Researches external sources and returns a sourced Korean report: library documentation and API references, version and migration notes, technology comparisons, best practices, and troubleshooting of external issues. Use it as the research step before brainstorming, design, or documentation, or when a decision depends on current external information; run it in parallel with the Explore agent when the internal codebase also matters. Read-only. ([jo-minjun/dotfiles](https://github.com/jo-minjun/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Deep web research subagent for the /research skill. Investigates a single research angle by searching the web, evaluating sources, and returning a distilled structured summary with citations. Launched in parallel batches of 4-6 by the research orchestrator. &lt;example&gt; Context: The /research skill has decomposed a topic into research angles and is dispatching subagents. assistant: "Launching web-researcher to investigate authentication patterns for WebAuthn." &lt;commentary&gt; Each research angle gets its own web-researcher subagent running in the background. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([kelsi-andrewss/claude-multi-agent-pipeline](https://github.com/kelsi-andrewss/claude-multi-agent-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: General-purpose web research agent. Searches the internet via DuckDuckGo and reads pages as plain text. Use when you need real, grounded information and no llms.txt is available — library APIs, error messages, best practices, migration guides, comparisons, or any question that needs current web sources. Returns findings with citations. Lightweight: no browser, no API keys. ([Kolsetu-Opensource/loop-engg-voice-ai](https://github.com/Kolsetu-Opensource/loop-engg-voice-ai/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Finds facts outside the repository for a sprint session: official docs, specs, first-party APIs. Works from primary sources, cites every claim and saves nothing. Never decides and never talks to the user. The filled prompt is the complete task. ([likeahuman-ai/development-advanced](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/development-advanced/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)

- **web-researcher**: Runs ONE search pass of a research run for the research or quick-research skill: finds candidate source URLs with the assigned search services, in the given source languages, and returns them as JSON lines. No page text in the result. Started by the skill, which names the pass, the languages, the queries and the services. For a quick single fact the main session asks Parallel or Exa directly, not this agent. ALWAYS spawn as a plain subagent, never as a named teammate: an in-process named teammate ignores this file's mcpServers, so the agent would run without its search services. - **web-researcher**: Internet research worker. Use it instead of running web searches or fetching pages yourself, whenever a task needs information from the internet. Give it the question, why it's needed, and a depth (quick for one fact, medium, or thorough). It plans the search, vets each source for quality and recency, reads the actual pages and video… ([WakaChobii/claude-arsenal](https://github.com/WakaChobii/claude-arsenal/blob/HEAD/plugins/research-team/agents/web-researcher.md))
- **web-researcher**: Web 検索と Web ページの取得による情報収集を担当する。ライブラリやツールの仕様、公式ドキュメント、リリースノート、既知の不具合などを調べ、出典付きで報告する。リポジトリやローカルのファイルの調査には使わない(helper の担当)。 ([yash268925/dotclaude](https://github.com/yash268925/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/agents/web-researcher.md))
- **web-researcher**: Multi-page web reader that returns conclusions, never raw pages. Pass a research question plus any seed URLs. The agent discovers pages with WebSearch, walks them via token-bounded `ccx web` views, and returns synthesized findings with a `&lt;url&gt; §ref` cite per claim. Spawn it for research spanning several pages or sites — the cross-page lane the ccx guard messages point at. One question about one page is cc-context:web-fetch's lane. ([yasyf/cc-context](https://github.com/yasyf/cc-context/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/web-researcher.md))
- **web-researcher**: Web を検索・閲覧して必要な情報を調べ、出典つきで報告するエージェント。ライブラリ・API の仕様、最新バージョン、技術比較、エラーメッセージの原因調査など、リポジトリ内では分からない外部情報が必要なときに使う。コードやファイルは変更しない。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 実装前にライブラリの仕様を確認したい。 user: "このライブラリの最新バージョンの認証 API の使い方を調べて" assistant: "web-researcherエージェントで公式ドキュメントを調べて報告します" &lt;/example&gt; ([yfujita/fess-suggest-workspace](https://github.com/yfujita/fess-suggest-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **web-researcher**: 웹에서 기술 주제에 대한 포괄적인 리서치를 수행하여 다중 플랫폼(Reddit, GitHub, Stack Overflow, Hugging Face, arXiv 등)에서 정보를 수집하고 종합 보고서를 생성해야 할 때 이 에이전트를 사용하세요. ([YoungjaeDev/tarogak](https://github.com/YoungjaeDev/tarogak/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/web-researcher.md))

- **webpage-designer**: Web design lead for static/marketing sites. Use FIRST for any visual or UX change (new page, new section, redesign, theming, copy layout, design audit). Produces a written design plan for human approval and owns the shared design system (tokens, theme, style docs). Does not write components, pages or scripts — that is webpage-coder's job after the plan is approved. ([molayab/web-agentic-pipeline](https://github.com/molayab/web-agentic-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-agentic-pipeline/agents/webpage-designer.md))
- **webpage-publisher**: Release step for static/Astro sites. Use AFTER webpage-coder marked a plan implemented AND the human asked to publish. Re-verifies the build and acceptance criteria, then commits on a feature branch and opens (or updates) a pull request. Never merges, deploys or changes repository/hosting settings. ([molayab/web-agentic-pipeline](https://github.com/molayab/web-agentic-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-agentic-pipeline/agents/webpage-publisher.md))
- **webpage-security-reviewer**: Security gate for static/Astro sites. Use AFTER webpage-coder marked a plan implemented (and after any approved SEO changes were applied) and BEFORE the human decides to publish. Reviews the pending changes for leaked secrets, PII and unsafe code, and checks dependency versions for known vulnerabilities and supply-chain risk. Read-only on the codebase; records a PASS/FAIL verdict in the plan. Never fixes code, commits or publishes. ([molayab/web-agentic-pipeline](https://github.com/molayab/web-agentic-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-agentic-pipeline/agents/webpage-security-reviewer.md))
- **webpage-seo-reviewer**: SEO gate for static/Astro sites. Use AFTER webpage-coder marked a plan implemented and BEFORE the security review. Mode "audit" is read-only: it checks on-page and technical SEO, structured data, hreflang, sitemap, feeds and content freshness, and proposes updates in the plan. Mode "apply" runs only after the human approved the proposals and applies exactly those. Never invents product claims or metrics, never touches legal copy, never commits or publishes. ([molayab/web-agentic-pipeline](https://github.com/molayab/web-agentic-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-agentic-pipeline/agents/webpage-seo-reviewer.md))
